- Noto Sans Mono CJK SC: Referenced as a CJK monospace fallback, but not available through Google Fonts CSS2. Keep an explicit fallback stack such as JetBrains Mono, Noto Sans SC, monospace until a licensed local source is added.
- Smiley Sans Oblique: Referenced for Chinese display adaptation and named as 得意黑. Not available through Google Fonts CSS2; do not vendor from an unverified CDN without checking license and provenance.
- Yozai: Referenced as 悠哉字体 from cn-fontsource in CJK adaptation notes. Not available through Google Fonts CSS2; add only from a verified licensed source.
